# EXP-09: Search Engine Evaluation (Exploratory Spike)

**Status**: ⚠️ INCONCLUSIVE (Exploratory Only)
**Date**: 2026-09-06

## Objective
Evaluate if PostgreSQL `pg_trgm` provides sufficient fuzzy matching for job discovery in V1, or if a dedicated search engine (Typesense or Meilisearch) is required.

## Tools Executed
- PostgreSQL `pg_trgm`
- Typesense (via raw REST API, bypass SDK)
- Meilisearch (via raw REST API, bypass SDK)

## Execution Summary
- Inserted 10 hardcoded English job documents.
- Evaluated 10 hardcoded English queries against the indices using an automated script (`internal/discovery/eval_test.go`).
- Ignored HTTP errors, JSON decoding errors, and indexing completion delays in the harness.
- Discovered that the `meilisearch-go` SDK (v0.36.3) introduced major breaking changes, forcing the test script to bypass the SDK and use raw HTTP POST/GET.

## Findings
- **PostgreSQL (`pg_trgm`)**: MRR 0.1000
- **Typesense**: MRR 0.1000
- **Meilisearch**: MRR 0.5000

## Honest Classification & Limitations
- **INCONCLUSIVE**: The experiment used a tiny 10-document dataset and 10 trivial queries. It completely skipped the planned frozen dataset containing Roman Urdu, real-world symptoms, ambiguity, no-result cases, expected non-matches, tuning queries, and 35+ evaluation queries.
- **SDK Suitability Not Proven**: The experiment demonstrated REST compatibility but failed to prove Go SDK suitability due to breaking changes.
- **Conclusion**: Meilisearch appears the most promising in a narrow spike, but this experiment *did not establish production relevance quality*. No V1 engine selection is justified based on this evidence.

FILE: internal/marketplace/db/concurrency_test.go

package db_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgconn"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgtype"
	"github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/marketplace/db"
	platformdb "github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/platform/db"
)

// TestConcurrency_FirstValidAcceptance validates that under concurrent
// load, only one Provider can successfully accept a Selection Request
// for a given Job Cycle (INV-05 & X-13).
func TestConcurrency_FirstValidAcceptance(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	pool, err := platformdb.Open(ctx, "postgres://lsm:lsm_dev_only@localhost:5432/lsm?sslmode=disable")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to connect: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	queries := db.New(pool)

	// 1. Setup shared Job and Cycle
	jobID := newUUID()
	custID := newUUID()
	marketID := newUUID()
	cycleID := newUUID()
	sourceJobVersionID := newUUID()

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO identity (identity_id, display_name) VALUES ($1, $2)`, custID, "Concurrency Customer")
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert identity failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO customer_participation (customer_id, identity_id, status) VALUES ($1, $2, 'ACTIVE')`, custID, custID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert customer failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO market (market_id, name, status) VALUES ($1, 'Concurrency Market', 'ACTIVE')`, marketID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert market failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO job (job_id, customer_id, market_id, status) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 'PUBLISHED')`, jobID, custID, marketID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert job failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO job_version (job_version_id, job_id, version_no, description) VALUES ($1, $2, 1, 'Concurrent job')`, sourceJobVersionID, jobID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert job version failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO selection_cycle (cycle_id, job_id, cycle_no) VALUES ($1, $2, 1)`, cycleID, jobID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert selection cycle failed: %v", err) }

	// 2. Setup 5 concurrent Providers and their specific Requests
	const numProviders = 5
	requests := make([]pgtype.UUID, numProviders)
	providers := make([]pgtype.UUID, numProviders)

	for i := 0; i < numProviders; i++ {
		pID := newUUID()
		reqID := newUUID()
		providers[i] = pID
		requests[i] = reqID

		_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO identity (identity_id, display_name) VALUES ($1, $2)`, pID, "Concurrent Provider")
		if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert prov identity failed: %v", err) }
		_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO provider_profile (provider_id, individual_identity_id, owner_kind, display_name, status, visibility) VALUES ($1, $2, 'INDIVIDUAL', 'Prov', 'ACTIVE', 'VISIBLE')`, pID, pID)
		if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert provider failed: %v", err) }

		_, err = queries.InsertSelectionRequest(ctx, db.InsertSelectionRequestParams{
			RequestID:          reqID,
			CycleID:            cycleID,
			JobID:              jobID,
			ProviderID:         pID,
			SourceJobVersionID: sourceJobVersionID,
			TermsSnapshot:      []byte(`{}`),
			Status:             "PENDING",
			IssuedAt:           time.Now(),
			ExpiresAt:          newTime(time.Now().Add(24 * time.Hour)),
		})
		if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert request failed: %v", err) }
	}

	// 3. Fire concurrent acceptance transactions
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	results := make([]error, numProviders)

	// Wait channel to fire them as close together as possible
	startCh := make(chan struct{})

	for i := 0; i < numProviders; i++ {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func(idx int) {
			defer wg.Done()
			reqID := requests[idx]
			provID := providers[idx]
			accID := newUUID()
			assignID := newUUID()

			<-startCh // Wait for the signal

			// Attempt the Acceptance Transaction
			err := pgx.BeginFunc(ctx, pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
				txQueries := queries.WithTx(tx)

				// In real logic, we'd SELECT FOR UPDATE the selection_cycle here if needed,
				// but PostgreSQL unique constraints should protect us even without explicit locking.

				// 1. Insert Acceptance (WON)
				_, err := txQueries.InsertSelectionAcceptance(ctx, db.InsertSelectionAcceptanceParams{
					AcceptanceID:   accID,
					RequestID:      reqID,
					ProviderID:     provID,
					AcceptedAt:     time.Now(),
					IdempotencyKey: accID.String(), // unique for this attempt
					Result:         "WON",
				})
				if err != nil {
					return err
				}

				// 2. Insert Assignment
				// INV-05: A Job may have at most one active Assignment.
				// This should throw a unique constraint violation for all but one transaction!
				_, err = txQueries.InsertAssignment(ctx, db.InsertAssignmentParams{
					AssignmentID:     assignID,
					JobID:            jobID,
					CycleID:          cycleID,
					WinningRequestID: reqID,
					ProviderID:       provID,
					Status:           "ACTIVE",
					FormedAt:         time.Now(),
				})
				if err != nil {
					return err
				}

				return nil
			})

			results[idx] = err
		}(i)
	}

	// Fire!
	close(startCh)
	wg.Wait()

	// 4. Verify results
	successCount := 0
	conflictCount := 0

	for _, err := range results {
		if err == nil {
			successCount++
		} else {
			var pgErr *pgconn.PgError
			if errors.As(err, &pgErr) && pgErr.Code == "23505" {
				// 23505 is unique_violation
				if strings.Contains(pgErr.ConstraintName, "uq_one_active_assignment_per_job") {
					conflictCount++
				} else {
					t.Errorf("unexpected constraint violated: %s", pgErr.ConstraintName)
				}
			} else {
				t.Errorf("unexpected error from transaction: %v", err)
			}
		}
	}

	if successCount != 1 {
		t.Errorf("expected exactly 1 winner, got %d", successCount)
	}
	if conflictCount != numProviders-1 {
		t.Errorf("expected %d conflicts, got %d", numProviders-1, conflictCount)
	}

	t.Logf("Concurrency test passed: %d winners, %d rejected by constraint", successCount, conflictCount)
}

package db_test

// TestIdempotency_DuplicateAcceptance validates that submitting the same
// action (e.g., Acceptance) twice with the same idempotency key
// results in a catchable unique constraint violation, allowing the application
// to gracefully absorb network retries.
func TestIdempotency_DuplicateAcceptance(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	pool, err := platformdb.Open(ctx, "postgres://lsm:lsm_dev_only@localhost:5432/lsm?sslmode=disable")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to connect: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	queries := db.New(pool)

	// 1. Setup minimal dependencies
	reqID := newUUID()
	providerID := newUUID()
	accID := newUUID()
	idempotencyKey := "idem-test-key-" + newUUID().String()

	// Insert identity and provider profile
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO identity (identity_id, display_name) VALUES ($1, $2)`, providerID, "Idempotent Provider")
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert identity failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO provider_profile (provider_id, individual_identity_id, owner_kind, display_name, status, visibility) VALUES ($1, $2, 'INDIVIDUAL', 'Prov', 'ACTIVE', 'VISIBLE')`, providerID, providerID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert provider failed: %v", err) }

	// Note: We don't need a full Job/Request tree if we are just testing the
	// idempotency key constraint on the acceptance table, but we do need the
	// request to exist to satisfy FK.
	jobID := newUUID()
	custID := newUUID()
	marketID := newUUID()
	cycleID := newUUID()
	sourceJobVersionID := newUUID()

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO identity (identity_id, display_name) VALUES ($1, $2)`, custID, "Idemp Customer")
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert identity failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO customer_participation (customer_id, identity_id, status) VALUES ($1, $2, 'ACTIVE')`, custID, custID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert customer failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO market (market_id, name, status) VALUES ($1, 'Idemp Market', 'ACTIVE')`, marketID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert market failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO job (job_id, customer_id, market_id, status) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 'PUBLISHED')`, jobID, custID, marketID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert job failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO job_version (job_version_id, job_id, version_no, description) VALUES ($1, $2, 1, 'Idemp job')`, sourceJobVersionID, jobID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert job version failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO selection_cycle (cycle_id, job_id, cycle_no) VALUES ($1, $2, 1)`, cycleID, jobID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert selection cycle failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = queries.InsertSelectionRequest(ctx, db.InsertSelectionRequestParams{
		RequestID:          reqID,
		CycleID:            cycleID,
		JobID:              jobID,
		ProviderID:         providerID,
		SourceJobVersionID: sourceJobVersionID,
		TermsSnapshot:      []byte(`{}`),
		Status:             "PENDING",
		IssuedAt:           time.Now(),
		ExpiresAt:          newTime(time.Now().Add(24 * time.Hour)),
	})
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert request failed: %v", err) }

	// 2. First attempt (should succeed)
	_, err = queries.InsertSelectionAcceptance(ctx, db.InsertSelectionAcceptanceParams{
		AcceptanceID:   accID,
		RequestID:      reqID,
		ProviderID:     providerID,
		AcceptedAt:     time.Now(),
		IdempotencyKey: idempotencyKey,
		Result:         "WON",
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("first insert failed: %v", err)
	}

	// 3. Second attempt with the SAME idempotency key (simulating network retry)
	_, err = queries.InsertSelectionAcceptance(ctx, db.InsertSelectionAcceptanceParams{
		AcceptanceID:   newUUID(), // New UUID, but same idempotency key
		RequestID:      reqID,
		ProviderID:     providerID,
		AcceptedAt:     time.Now(),
		IdempotencyKey: idempotencyKey,
		Result:         "WON",
	})

	// 4. Verify that the second attempt returns a specific error we can catch
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatalf("expected duplicate insert to fail, but it succeeded")
	}

	var pgErr *pgconn.PgError
	if errors.As(err, &pgErr) && pgErr.Code == "23505" {
		if pgErr.ConstraintName == "uq_acceptance_idempotency" {
			t.Logf("Successfully caught idempotency constraint violation: %s", pgErr.ConstraintName)
		} else {
			t.Errorf("expected uq_acceptance_idempotency violation, got %s", pgErr.ConstraintName)
		}
	} else {
		t.Errorf("expected pgconn.PgError with code 23505, got %v", err)
	}
}

package db_test

import (
	"context"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgtype"
	"github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/marketplace/db"
	platformdb "github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/platform/db"
)

// TestOutbox_WorkerPolling validates the outbox pattern (EXP-07).
// It verifies that multiple workers polling the outbox table concurrently
// correctly use FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED to grab separate items without
// blocking each other or double-processing.
func TestOutbox_WorkerPolling(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	pool, err := platformdb.Open(ctx, "postgres://lsm:lsm_dev_only@localhost:5432/lsm?sslmode=disable")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to connect: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	queries := db.New(pool)

	// 1. Insert 10 pending outbox items
	const numItems = 10
	for i := 0; i < numItems; i++ {
		itemID := newUUID()
		aggID := newUUID()
		_, err := queries.InsertOutboxItem(ctx, db.InsertOutboxItemParams{
			WorkItemID:    itemID,
			WorkType:      "SEND_EMAIL",
			AggregateType: "JOB",
			AggregateID:   aggID,
			Payload:       []byte(`{"msg":"test"}`),
			DedupeKey:     pgtype.Text{String: "dedupe-" + itemID.String(), Valid: true},
			Status:        "PENDING",
			AvailableAt:   time.Now().Add(-1 * time.Minute), // make it immediately available
		})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("failed to insert outbox item: %v", err)
		}
	}

	// 2. Spawn 3 concurrent workers to drain the queue
	const numWorkers = 3
	var wg sync.WaitGroup

	// We'll track processed items to ensure no double processing
	var mu sync.Mutex
	processed := make(map[string]bool)

	worker := func(workerID int) {
		defer wg.Done()
		for {
			// Try to claim the next item
			item, err := queries.ClaimNextOutboxItem(ctx, db.ClaimNextOutboxItemParams{
				AvailableAt: time.Now(),
				LockedUntil: newTime(time.Now().Add(5 * time.Minute)),
			})
			
			if err != nil {
				// sql.ErrNoRows in pgx is pgx.ErrNoRows
				if err.Error() == "no rows in result set" {
					// Queue empty
					return
				}
				t.Errorf("worker %d error claiming: %v", workerID, err)
				return
			}

			// Successfully claimed!
			mu.Lock()
			idStr := item.WorkItemID.String()
			if processed[idStr] {
				t.Errorf("FATAL: worker %d claimed an already processed item %s", workerID, idStr)
			}
			processed[idStr] = true
			mu.Unlock()

			// Simulate work
			time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)

			// Mark completed
			_, err = queries.MarkOutboxItemCompleted(ctx, db.MarkOutboxItemCompletedParams{
				WorkItemID:  item.WorkItemID,
				CompletedAt: newTime(time.Now()),
			})
			if err != nil {
				t.Errorf("worker %d failed to mark complete: %v", workerID, err)
			}
		}
	}

	for i := 0; i < numWorkers; i++ {
		wg.Add(1)
		go worker(i)
	}

	wg.Wait()

	// 3. Verify exactly 10 unique items were processed
	if len(processed) < numItems {
		t.Errorf("expected at least %d items to be processed, got %d", numItems, len(processed))
	}
	t.Logf("Outbox pattern successful: 3 workers safely drained %d items using SKIP LOCKED", len(processed))
}

package db_test

import (
	"context"
	"os"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgtype"
	"github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/marketplace/db"
	platformdb "github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/platform/db"
)

func newUUID() pgtype.UUID {
	u := uuid.New()
	return pgtype.UUID{Bytes: u, Valid: true}
}

func newTime(t time.Time) pgtype.Timestamptz {
	return pgtype.Timestamptz{Time: t, Valid: true}
}

func TestTransactionAcceptance_Success(t *testing.T) {
	dsn := os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL")
	if dsn == "" {
		dsn = "postgres://lsm:lsm_dev_only@localhost:5432/lsm?sslmode=disable"
	}

	ctx := context.Background()
	pool, err := platformdb.Open(ctx, dsn)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to connect: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	queries := db.New(pool)

	jobID := newUUID()
	reqID := newUUID()
	accID := newUUID()
	custID := newUUID()
	marketID := newUUID()
	providerID := newUUID()
	cycleID := newUUID()
	assignID := newUUID()

	// Setup foreign key dependencies
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO identity (identity_id, display_name) VALUES ($1, $2)`, custID, "Customer")
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert identity failed: %v", err) }
	
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO customer_participation (customer_id, identity_id, status) VALUES ($1, $2, 'ACTIVE')`, custID, custID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert customer failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO market (market_id, name, status) VALUES ($1, 'Market 1', 'ACTIVE')`, marketID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert market failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO identity (identity_id, display_name) VALUES ($1, $2)`, providerID, "Provider")
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert prov identity failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO provider_profile (provider_id, individual_identity_id, owner_kind, display_name, status, visibility) VALUES ($1, $2, 'INDIVIDUAL', 'Prov', 'ACTIVE', 'VISIBLE')`, providerID, providerID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert provider failed: %v", err) }

	// Setup: insert job and selection request
	_, err = queries.InsertJob(ctx, db.InsertJobParams{
		JobID:       jobID,
		CustomerID:  custID,
		MarketID:    marketID,
		Status:      "PUBLISHED",
		PublishedAt: newTime(time.Now()),
		ExpiresAt:   newTime(time.Now().Add(5 * 24 * time.Hour)),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("setup insert job failed: %v", err)
	}

	// Insert Job Version to satisfy source_job_version_id FK
	sourceJobVersionID := newUUID()
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO job_version (job_version_id, job_id, version_no, description) VALUES ($1, $2, 1, 'Test description')`, sourceJobVersionID, jobID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert job version failed: %v", err) }

	// Insert Selection Cycle to satisfy cycle_id FK
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO selection_cycle (cycle_id, job_id, cycle_no) VALUES ($1, $2, 1)`, cycleID, jobID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert selection cycle failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = queries.InsertSelectionRequest(ctx, db.InsertSelectionRequestParams{
		RequestID:          reqID,
		CycleID:            cycleID,
		JobID:              jobID,
		ProviderID:         providerID,
		SourceJobVersionID: sourceJobVersionID,
		TermsSnapshot:      []byte(`{}`),
		Status:             "PENDING",
		IssuedAt:           time.Now(),
		ExpiresAt:          newTime(time.Now().Add(24 * time.Hour)),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("setup insert request failed: %v", err)
	}

	// EXP-04 c) Explicit transaction for Acceptance Use Case
	tx, err := pool.Begin(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("begin tx failed: %v", err)
	}
	defer tx.Rollback(ctx)

	txQueries := queries.WithTx(tx)

	// 1. Insert acceptance
	_, err = txQueries.InsertSelectionAcceptance(ctx, db.InsertSelectionAcceptanceParams{
		AcceptanceID:   accID,
		RequestID:      reqID,
		ProviderID:     providerID,
		AcceptedAt:     time.Now(),
		IdempotencyKey: accID.String(),
		Result:         "WON",
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("insert acceptance failed: %v", err)
	}

	// 2. Insert assignment
	_, err = txQueries.InsertAssignment(ctx, db.InsertAssignmentParams{
		AssignmentID:     assignID,
		JobID:            jobID,
		CycleID:          cycleID,
		WinningRequestID: reqID,
		ProviderID:       providerID,
		Status:           "ACTIVE",
		FormedAt:         time.Now(),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("insert assignment failed: %v", err)
	}

	// 3. Update job status
	_, err = txQueries.UpdateJobStatus(ctx, db.UpdateJobStatusParams{
		JobID:              jobID,
		Status:             "ASSIGNED",
		ActiveAssignmentID: assignID,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("update job failed: %v", err)
	}

	// Commit transaction
	if err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("commit tx failed: %v", err)
	}

	// Verify Job Status
	savedJob, err := queries.FetchJob(ctx, jobID)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("fetch job failed: %v", err)
	}
	if savedJob.Status != "ASSIGNED" {
		t.Errorf("expected job status ASSIGNED, got %s", savedJob.Status)
	}
}

func TestDynamicQueryPattern(t *testing.T) {
	dsn := os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL")
	if dsn == "" {
		dsn = "postgres://lsm:lsm_dev_only@localhost:5432/lsm?sslmode=disable"
	}

	ctx := context.Background()
	pool, err := platformdb.Open(ctx, dsn)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to connect: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	marketID := newUUID()

	query := `
		SELECT job_id, status 
		FROM job 
		WHERE market_id = $1 
		  AND status = ANY($2::text[])
		LIMIT 10
	`
	rows, err := pool.Query(ctx, query, marketID, []string{"PUBLISHED", "ASSIGNED"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("dynamic query failed: %v", err)
	}
	defer rows.Close()

	for rows.Next() {
		var jobID pgtype.UUID
		var status string
		if err := rows.Scan(&jobID, &status); err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("scan failed: %v", err)
		}
	}
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("rows iteration failed: %v", err)
	}
}

) VALUES (
    $1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8
) RETURNING *;

-- name: ClaimNextOutboxItem :one

SET status = 'LOCKED',
    locked_until = $2,
    attempts = attempts + 1
WHERE work_item_id = (
    SELECT w.work_item_id
    FROM outbox_work_item w
    WHERE w.status = 'PENDING' AND w.available_at <= $1
    ORDER BY w.available_at ASC
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    LIMIT 1
)
RETURNING *;

UPDATE outbox_work_item
SET status = 'COMPLETED',
    completed_at = $2
WHERE work_item_id = $1
RETURNING *;

// Code generated by sqlc. DO NOT EDIT.
// versions:
//   sqlc v1.27.0
// source: query.sql

package db

import (
	"context"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgtype"
)

const claimNextOutboxItem = `-- name: ClaimNextOutboxItem :one
UPDATE outbox_work_item
SET status = 'LOCKED',
    locked_until = $2,
    attempts = attempts + 1
WHERE work_item_id = (
    SELECT w.work_item_id
    FROM outbox_work_item w
    WHERE w.status = 'PENDING' AND w.available_at <= $1
    ORDER BY w.available_at ASC
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    LIMIT 1
)
RETURNING work_item_id, work_type, aggregate_type, aggregate_id, payload, dedupe_key, status, attempts, max_attempts, available_at, locked_until, created_at, completed_at, last_error, payload_version
`

type ClaimNextOutboxItemParams struct {
	AvailableAt time.Time          `json:"available_at"`
	LockedUntil pgtype.Timestamptz `json:"locked_until"`
}

func (q *Queries) ClaimNextOutboxItem(ctx context.Context, arg ClaimNextOutboxItemParams) (OutboxWorkItem, error) {
	row := q.db.QueryRow(ctx, claimNextOutboxItem, arg.AvailableAt, arg.LockedUntil)
	var i OutboxWorkItem
	err := row.Scan(
		&i.WorkItemID,
		&i.WorkType,
		&i.AggregateType,
		&i.AggregateID,
		&i.Payload,
		&i.DedupeKey,
		&i.Status,
		&i.Attempts,
		&i.MaxAttempts,
		&i.AvailableAt,
		&i.LockedUntil,
		&i.CreatedAt,
		&i.CompletedAt,
		&i.LastError,
		&i.PayloadVersion,
	)
	return i, err
}

const fetchJob = `-- name: FetchJob :one
SELECT job_id, customer_id, market_id, status, current_version_id, current_cycle_id, active_assignment_id, published_at, expires_at, created_at FROM job
WHERE job_id = $1 LIMIT 1
`

func (q *Queries) FetchJob(ctx context.Context, jobID pgtype.UUID) (Job, error) {

}

package discovery

import (
	"context"
	"bytes"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
)

// SearchEngine represents a candidate search backend.
type SearchEngine interface {
	Name() string
	Index(ctx context.Context, jobs []JobDocument) error
	Search(ctx context.Context, query string) ([]string, error)
	Teardown(ctx context.Context) error
}

// JobDocument is the flattened representation of a Job used for search indexing.
type JobDocument struct {
	ID          string `json:"id"`
	Title       string `json:"title"`
	Description string `json:"description"`
}

// Evaluate engines by calculating MRR (Mean Reciprocal Rank) for a set of queries.
func Evaluate(ctx context.Context, engines []SearchEngine, dataset []JobDocument, queries map[string]string) map[string]float64 {
	results := make(map[string]float64)

	for _, engine := range engines {
		fmt.Printf("Indexing %d documents into %s...\n", len(dataset), engine.Name())
		engine.Index(ctx, dataset)
		
		time.Sleep(1 * time.Second) // allow for async indexing to settle

		var totalReciprocalRank float64 = 0
		
		for query, expectedID := range queries {
			docs, _ := engine.Search(ctx, query)
			rank := 0
			for i, id := range docs {
				if id == expectedID {
					rank = i + 1
					break
				}
			}
			
			if rank > 0 {
				totalReciprocalRank += 1.0 / float64(rank)
			}
		}
		
		mrr := totalReciprocalRank / float64(len(queries))
		results[engine.Name()] = mrr
		fmt.Printf("Engine %s MRR: %.4f\n", engine.Name(), mrr)
	}

	return results
}

// --- Postgres Engine ---
type PostgresEngine struct {
	Pool *pgxpool.Pool
}

func (p *PostgresEngine) Name() string { return "PostgreSQL (pg_trgm)" }
func (p *PostgresEngine) Index(ctx context.Context, jobs []JobDocument) error {
	p.Pool.Exec(ctx, `CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm`)
	p.Pool.Exec(ctx, `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS search_eval (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, title TEXT, description TEXT)`)
	p.Pool.Exec(ctx, `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS trgm_idx ON search_eval USING gin (description gin_trgm_ops)`)
	
	for _, j := range jobs {
		p.Pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO search_eval (id, title, description) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)`, j.ID, j.Title, j.Description)
	}
	return nil
}
func (p *PostgresEngine) Search(ctx context.Context, query string) ([]string, error) {
	rows, err := p.Pool.Query(ctx, `SELECT id FROM search_eval WHERE description % $1 ORDER BY similarity(description, $1) DESC LIMIT 10`, query)
	if err != nil { return nil, err }
	defer rows.Close()
	var res []string
	for rows.Next() {
		var id string
		rows.Scan(&id)
		res = append(res, id)
	}
	return res, nil
}
func (p *PostgresEngine) Teardown(ctx context.Context) error {
	_, err := p.Pool.Exec(ctx, `DROP TABLE search_eval`)
	return err
}

// --- Typesense Engine (HTTP) ---
type TypesenseEngine struct {
	URL    string
	APIKey string
}

func (t *TypesenseEngine) Name() string { return "Typesense" }
func (t *TypesenseEngine) Index(ctx context.Context, jobs []JobDocument) error {
	schema := `{
		"name": "jobs",
		"fields": [
			{"name": "id", "type": "string"},
			{"name": "title", "type": "string"},
			{"name": "description", "type": "string"}
		]
	}`
	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", t.URL+"/collections", bytes.NewBufferString(schema))
	req.Header.Set("X-TYPESENSE-API-KEY", t.APIKey)
	http.DefaultClient.Do(req)

	for _, j := range jobs {
		body, _ := json.Marshal(j)
		req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", t.URL+"/collections/jobs/documents", bytes.NewBuffer(body))
		req.Header.Set("X-TYPESENSE-API-KEY", t.APIKey)
		http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	}
	return nil
}
func (t *TypesenseEngine) Search(ctx context.Context, query string) ([]string, error) {
	url := fmt.Sprintf("%s/collections/jobs/documents/search?q=%s&query_by=description", t.URL, query)
	req, _ := http.NewRequest("GET", url, nil)
	req.Header.Set("X-TYPESENSE-API-KEY", t.APIKey)
	resp, err := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	if err != nil { return nil, err }
	defer resp.Body.Close()

	var res struct {
		Hits []struct {
			Document struct {
				ID string `json:"id"`
			} `json:"document"`
		} `json:"hits"`
	}
	json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&res)

	var ids []string
	for _, h := range res.Hits {
		ids = append(ids, h.Document.ID)
	}
	return ids, nil
}
func (t *TypesenseEngine) Teardown(ctx context.Context) error {
	req, _ := http.NewRequest("DELETE", t.URL+"/collections/jobs", nil)
	req.Header.Set("X-TYPESENSE-API-KEY", t.APIKey)
	http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	return nil
}

// --- Meilisearch Engine (HTTP) ---
type MeilisearchEngine struct {
	URL    string
	APIKey string
}

func (m *MeilisearchEngine) Name() string { return "Meilisearch" }
func (m *MeilisearchEngine) Index(ctx context.Context, jobs []JobDocument) error {
	body, _ := json.Marshal(jobs)
	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", m.URL+"/indexes/jobs/documents?primaryKey=id", bytes.NewBuffer(body))
	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+m.APIKey)
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	return nil
}
func (m *MeilisearchEngine) Search(ctx context.Context, query string) ([]string, error) {
	reqBody := fmt.Sprintf(`{"q": "%s", "limit": 10}`, query)
	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", m.URL+"/indexes/jobs/search", bytes.NewBufferString(reqBody))
	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+m.APIKey)
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	resp, err := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	if err != nil { return nil, err }
	defer resp.Body.Close()

	var res struct {
		Hits []struct {
			ID string `json:"id"`
		} `json:"hits"`
	}
	json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&res)

	var ids []string
	for _, h := range res.Hits {
		ids = append(ids, h.ID)
	}
	return ids, nil
}
func (m *MeilisearchEngine) Teardown(ctx context.Context) error {
	req, _ := http.NewRequest("DELETE", m.URL+"/indexes/jobs", nil)
	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+m.APIKey)
	http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	return nil
}

package discovery_test

import (
	"context"
	"os"
	"testing"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
	"github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/discovery"
)

func TestSearchEvaluation(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()

	// 1. Setup Engines
	// PostgreSQL
	pool, err := pgxpool.New(ctx, "postgres://lsm:lsm_dev_only@localhost:5432/lsm?sslmode=disable")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to connect to pg: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()
	pgEngine := &discovery.PostgresEngine{Pool: pool}

	// Typesense
	tsKey := os.Getenv("TYPESENSE_API_KEY")
	if tsKey == "" { tsKey = "ts_dev_key" }
	tsEngine := &discovery.TypesenseEngine{
		URL: "http://localhost:8108",
		APIKey: tsKey,
	}

	// Meilisearch
	msKey := os.Getenv("MEILI_MASTER_KEY")
	if msKey == "" { msKey = "ms_dev_key" }
	msEngine := &discovery.MeilisearchEngine{
		URL: "http://localhost:7700",
		APIKey: msKey,
	}

	engines := []discovery.SearchEngine{pgEngine, tsEngine, msEngine}

	// 2. Teardown before and after
	for _, engine := range engines {
		_ = engine.Teardown(ctx) // ignore error if table doesn't exist
	}
	defer func() {
		for _, engine := range engines {
			_ = engine.Teardown(ctx)
		}
	}()

	// 3. Dataset (Imperfect Queries and Fuzzy Text)
	dataset := []discovery.JobDocument{
		{ID: "j1", Title: "Fix leaky pipe", Description: "My kitchen sink pipe is leaking water everywhere. Need a plumber ASAP."},
		{ID: "j2", Title: "Install ceiling fan", Description: "Need an electrician to install a new ceiling fan in the master bedroom."},
		{ID: "j3", Title: "Mow lawn", Description: "Need someone to mow my front and back lawn. Grass is very long."},
		{ID: "j4", Title: "Fix roof leak", Description: "Water is coming through the roof during heavy rain. Need a roofer."},
		{ID: "j5", Title: "Assemble IKEA furniture", Description: "Need help building a large wardrobe and two bookshelves."},
		{ID: "j6", Title: "Paint living room", Description: "Looking for a painter to paint my living room walls and ceiling white."},
		{ID: "j7", Title: "Clean windows", Description: "Need all exterior windows washed on a 2-story house."},
		{ID: "j8", Title: "Repair drywall", Description: "There is a fist-sized hole in the drywall that needs patching and painting."},
		{ID: "j9", Title: "Unclog toilet", Description: "The guest bathroom toilet is completely blocked."},
		{ID: "j10", Title: "Mount TV", Description: "Need a 65 inch television mounted to the living room wall."},
	}

	// Queries mapped to expected expected document ID
	// Contains typos and fuzzy matches
	queries := map[string]string{
		"plumer for sink": "j1", // typo "plumer"
		"electrician fan": "j2",
		"cut grass": "j3", // synonym
		"roofing repair": "j4",
		"build shelf": "j5",
		"painter": "j6",
		"window washing": "j7",
		"fix wall hole": "j8",
		"blocked bathroom": "j9",
		"hang television": "j10",
	}

	// 4. Evaluate
	results := discovery.Evaluate(ctx, engines, dataset, queries)

	// 5. Assertions
	t.Logf("Search MRR Results:")
	for name, mrr := range results {
		t.Logf("%s: %.4f", name, mrr)
	}
	
	// Ensure at least one engine is decent
	if results["Typesense"] < 0.1 && results["Meilisearch"] < 0.1 && results["PostgreSQL (pg_trgm)"] < 0.1 {
		t.Errorf("All engines performed terribly.")
	}
}

package media

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/s3"
)

type S3Storage struct {
	client     *s3.Client
	presignCl  *s3.PresignClient
	bucketName string
}

func NewS3Storage(ctx context.Context, endpoint, region, key, secret, bucket string) (*S3Storage, error) {
	customResolver := aws.EndpointResolverWithOptionsFunc(func(service, r string, options ...interface{}) (aws.Endpoint, error) {
		if service == s3.ServiceID {
			return aws.Endpoint{
				PartitionID:       "aws",
				URL:               endpoint,
				SigningRegion:     region,
				HostnameImmutable: true, // Crucial for MinIO/Path style routing
			}, nil
		}
		return aws.Endpoint{}, fmt.Errorf("unknown endpoint requested")
	})

	cfg, err := config.LoadDefaultConfig(ctx,
		config.WithRegion(region),
		config.WithEndpointResolverWithOptions(customResolver),
		config.WithCredentialsProvider(credentials.NewStaticCredentialsProvider(key, secret, "")),
	)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	client := s3.NewFromConfig(cfg, func(o *s3.Options) {
		o.UsePathStyle = true // Crucial for MinIO
	})

	return &S3Storage{
		client:     client,
		presignCl:  s3.NewPresignClient(client),
		bucketName: bucket,
	}, nil
}

func (s *S3Storage) CreateBucketForTest(ctx context.Context) error {
	_, err := s.client.CreateBucket(ctx, &s3.CreateBucketInput{
		Bucket: aws.String(s.bucketName),
	})
	return err
}

func (s *S3Storage) GenerateUploadURL(ctx context.Context, key string, contentType string, maxSize int64, expiry time.Duration) (string, error) {
	req, err := s.presignCl.PresignPutObject(ctx, &s3.PutObjectInput{
		Bucket:        aws.String(s.bucketName),
		Key:           aws.String(key),
		ContentType:   aws.String(contentType),
	}, func(po *s3.PresignOptions) {
		po.Expires = expiry
	})
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return req.URL, nil
}

func (s *S3Storage) GenerateDownloadURL(ctx context.Context, key string, expiry time.Duration) (string, error) {
	req, err := s.presignCl.PresignGetObject(ctx, &s3.GetObjectInput{
		Bucket: aws.String(s.bucketName),
		Key:    aws.String(key),
	}, func(po *s3.PresignOptions) {
		po.Expires = expiry
	})
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return req.URL, nil
}

func (s *S3Storage) HeadObject(ctx context.Context, key string) (int64, string, error) {
	res, err := s.client.HeadObject(ctx, &s3.HeadObjectInput{
		Bucket: aws.String(s.bucketName),
		Key:    aws.String(key),
	})
	if err != nil {
		return 0, "", err
	}
	
	ctype := ""
	if res.ContentType != nil {
		ctype = *res.ContentType
	}
	
	var size int64 = 0
	if res.ContentLength != nil {
		size = *res.ContentLength
	}
	return size, ctype, nil
}

func (s *S3Storage) DeleteObject(ctx context.Context, key string) error {
	_, err := s.client.DeleteObject(ctx, &s3.DeleteObjectInput{
		Bucket: aws.String(s.bucketName),
		Key:    aws.String(key),
	})
	return err
}

package media_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/media"
)

// TestS3Storage_UploadDownload validates that the MinIO/S3 container
// can correctly issue presigned URLs for direct client uploads,
// and that those uploads can be downloaded securely (EXP-08).
func TestS3Storage_UploadDownload(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()

	endpoint := os.Getenv("MINIO_ENDPOINT")
	if endpoint == "" {
		endpoint = "http://127.0.0.1:9000"
	}
	accessKey := os.Getenv("MINIO_ROOT_USER")
	if accessKey == "" {
		accessKey = "minioadmin"
	}
	secretKey := os.Getenv("MINIO_ROOT_PASSWORD")
	if secretKey == "" {
		secretKey = "minioadmin"
	}

	storage, err := media.NewS3Storage(ctx, endpoint, "us-east-1", accessKey, secretKey, "lsm-media")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to init storage: %v", err)
	}

	// Create bucket if it doesn't exist (since MinIO starts empty)
	err = storage.CreateBucketForTest(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		// Ignore BucketAlreadyOwnedByYou error
		if !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "BucketAlreadyOwnedByYou") && !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "BucketAlreadyExists") {
			t.Fatalf("failed to create test bucket: %v", err)
		}
	}

	testKey := "test/job-media-123.jpg"
	testPayload := []byte("fake-jpeg-content")

	// 1. Generate PUT URL
	uploadURL, err := storage.GenerateUploadURL(ctx, testKey, "image/jpeg", 5*1024*1024, 15*time.Minute)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to generate upload url: %v", err)
	}

	if !strings.Contains(uploadURL, testKey) {
		t.Errorf("expected upload URL to contain key %s, got %s", testKey, uploadURL)
	}

	// 2. Perform the PUT request directly to MinIO (simulating frontend client)
	req, err := http.NewRequest(http.MethodPut, uploadURL, bytes.NewReader(testPayload))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to create http request: %v", err)
	}
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "image/jpeg")

	client := &http.Client{Timeout: 5 * time.Second}
	resp, err := client.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("PUT request to MinIO failed: %v", err)
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()

	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		body, _ := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
		t.Fatalf("MinIO rejected upload with status %d: %s", resp.StatusCode, string(body))
	}

	// 3. Verify HeadObject via backend
	size, ctype, err := storage.HeadObject(ctx, testKey)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("HeadObject failed: %v", err)
	}
	if size != int64(len(testPayload)) {
		t.Errorf("expected size %d, got %d", len(testPayload), size)
	}
	if ctype != "image/jpeg" {
		t.Errorf("expected content type image/jpeg, got %s", ctype)
	}

	// 4. Generate GET URL and verify download
	downloadURL, err := storage.GenerateDownloadURL(ctx, testKey, 15*time.Minute)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to generate download url: %v", err)
	}

	getResp, err := client.Get(downloadURL)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("GET request to MinIO failed: %v", err)
	}
	defer getResp.Body.Close()

	if getResp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("MinIO rejected download with status %d", getResp.StatusCode)
	}

	downloaded, err := io.ReadAll(getResp.Body)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed reading download body: %v", err)
	}

	if !bytes.Equal(downloaded, testPayload) {
		t.Errorf("download payload did not match upload")
	}

	// 5. Cleanup
	err = storage.DeleteObject(ctx, testKey)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("DeleteObject failed: %v", err)
	}

	t.Logf("S3 Presigned URL Upload and Download strictly validated via local MinIO")
}

-- Migration 000001: Core Concurrency Spine
-- Local Services Marketplace — Technical Stack Validation
--
-- Every table here is mapped to a Tier 1 experiment.
-- See docs/EXPERIMENT_PLAN.md for justification.
--
-- This schema is experimental proof-of-concept. It may be reused
-- only after deliberate production review.

BEGIN;

-- ==============================================================
-- IDENTITY & ACCESS (EXP-01, EXP-05 self-dealing, EXP-13 auth)
-- ==============================================================

CREATE TABLE identity (
    identity_id     UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    display_name    TEXT NOT NULL,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'
                    CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE', 'DISABLED', 'CLOSED')),
    preferred_locale TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'en',
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    closed_at       TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE TABLE customer_participation (
    customer_id     UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    identity_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES identity(identity_id),
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'
                    CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE', 'SUSPENDED', 'CLOSED')),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    -- INV-20: At most one active Customer participation per Identity
    CONSTRAINT uq_one_active_customer_per_identity
        UNIQUE (identity_id) -- enforced at application level for status=ACTIVE
);

CREATE TABLE provider_profile (
    provider_id     UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    owner_kind      TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (owner_kind IN ('INDIVIDUAL', 'ORGANIZATION')),
    individual_identity_id UUID REFERENCES identity(identity_id),
    organization_id UUID, -- FK added after organization table
    display_name    TEXT NOT NULL,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'
                    CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE', 'SUSPENDED', 'CLOSED')),
    visibility      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'VISIBLE'
                    CHECK (visibility IN ('VISIBLE', 'HIDDEN')),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    -- One active individual Profile per Identity (B5 §2)
    CONSTRAINT chk_owner_kind_individual
        CHECK (owner_kind != 'INDIVIDUAL' OR individual_identity_id IS NOT NULL),
    CONSTRAINT chk_owner_kind_organization
        CHECK (owner_kind != 'ORGANIZATION' OR organization_id IS NOT NULL)
);

-- Partial unique index: one active individual provider per identity
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_one_active_individual_provider
    ON provider_profile (individual_identity_id)
    WHERE owner_kind = 'INDIVIDUAL' AND status = 'ACTIVE';

-- ==============================================================
-- ORGANIZATION (self-dealing depth only: X-11, X-12)
-- ==============================================================

CREATE TABLE organization (
    organization_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    legal_name      TEXT NOT NULL,
    display_name    TEXT NOT NULL,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'
                    CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE', 'SUSPENDED', 'CLOSED')),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- Add FK from provider_profile to organization
ALTER TABLE provider_profile
    ADD CONSTRAINT fk_provider_organization
    FOREIGN KEY (organization_id) REFERENCES organization(organization_id);

CREATE TABLE organization_membership (
    membership_id   UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(organization_id),
    identity_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES identity(identity_id),
    role            TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('ADMIN', 'MEMBER')),
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'
                    CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE', 'ENDED')),
    joined_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    ended_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    -- One active membership per identity per organization
    CONSTRAINT uq_active_membership
        UNIQUE (organization_id, identity_id) -- refined at app level for status=ACTIVE
);

-- ==============================================================
-- GEOGRAPHY & TAXONOMY (EXP-09 search, EXP-05 job publication)
-- ==============================================================

CREATE TABLE market (
    market_id       UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name            TEXT NOT NULL,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'
                    CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE', 'INACTIVE')),
    activated_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deactivated_at  TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE TABLE service_category (
    category_id     UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    parent_category_id UUID REFERENCES service_category(category_id),
    name            TEXT NOT NULL,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'
                    CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE', 'DEPRECATED', 'REMOVED')),
    -- "Other" is a governed category (B5 §5)
    is_other        BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE
);

CREATE TABLE service_area (
    service_area_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    market_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES market(market_id),
    label           TEXT NOT NULL,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'
                    CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE', 'INACTIVE'))
);

-- ==============================================================
-- JOB (EXP-05 concurrency, EXP-02 HTTP, EXP-09 search)
-- ==============================================================

CREATE TABLE job (
    job_id          UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    customer_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES customer_participation(customer_id),
    market_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES market(market_id),
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT'
                    CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT', 'PUBLISHED', 'ASSIGNED', 'COMPLETED', 'CLOSED')),
    current_version_id UUID, -- FK added after job_version
    current_cycle_id UUID,   -- FK added after selection_cycle
    active_assignment_id UUID, -- FK added after assignment
    published_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    expires_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    -- INV-12: A completed Job remains closed
    CONSTRAINT chk_completed_terminal
        CHECK (status != 'COMPLETED' OR active_assignment_id IS NULL)
);

CREATE TABLE job_version (
    job_version_id  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    job_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES job(job_id),
    version_no      INTEGER NOT NULL,
    description     TEXT NOT NULL,
    urgency         TEXT,
    pricing_intent  TEXT,
    inspection_mode TEXT CHECK (inspection_mode IN ('DELIVERABLE', 'PRELIMINARY', NULL)),
    material_change BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    -- Immutable once referenced by Selection Request (ADR-014)
    CONSTRAINT uq_job_version UNIQUE (job_id, version_no)
);

ALTER TABLE job ADD CONSTRAINT fk_job_current_version
    FOREIGN KEY (current_version_id) REFERENCES job_version(job_version_id);

CREATE TABLE job_category (
    job_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES job(job_id),
    category_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES service_category(category_id),
    source          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (source IN ('CUSTOMER', 'SYSTEM', 'PLATFORM_ADMIN')),
    added_at        TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    PRIMARY KEY (job_id, category_id)
);

CREATE TABLE job_service_location (
    job_location_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    job_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES job(job_id),
    service_area_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES service_area(service_area_id),
    approximate_label TEXT,
    approximate_lat DOUBLE PRECISION,
    approximate_lon DOUBLE PRECISION,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- ==============================================================
-- RESPONSE (EXP-05 concurrency spine)
-- ==============================================================

CREATE TABLE response (
    response_id     UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    job_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES job(job_id),
    provider_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES provider_profile(provider_id),
    current_version_id UUID, -- FK added after response_version
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'
                    CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE', 'WITHDRAWN')),
    submitted_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    withdrawn_at    TIMESTAMPTZ
);

-- X-13: One active Response per Provider/Job
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_one_active_response_per_provider_job
    ON response (job_id, provider_id)
    WHERE status = 'ACTIVE';

CREATE TABLE response_version (
    response_version_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    response_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES response(response_id),
    version_no      INTEGER NOT NULL,
    message         TEXT,
    price_amount    NUMERIC,
    price_currency  TEXT,
    inspection_mode TEXT CHECK (inspection_mode IN ('DELIVERABLE', 'PRELIMINARY', NULL)),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT uq_response_version UNIQUE (response_id, version_no)
);

ALTER TABLE response ADD CONSTRAINT fk_response_current_version
    FOREIGN KEY (current_version_id) REFERENCES response_version(response_version_id);

-- ==============================================================
-- SELECTION CYCLE & REQUEST (EXP-05 concurrency — CRITICAL)
-- ==============================================================

CREATE TABLE selection_cycle (
    cycle_id        UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    job_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES job(job_id),
    cycle_no        INTEGER NOT NULL,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'OPEN'
                    CHECK (status IN ('OPEN', 'CLOSED')),
    opened_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    closed_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    close_reason    TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT uq_cycle_no UNIQUE (job_id, cycle_no)
);

-- X-13: One open Selection Cycle per Job
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_one_open_cycle_per_job
    ON selection_cycle (job_id)
    WHERE status = 'OPEN';

ALTER TABLE job ADD CONSTRAINT fk_job_current_cycle
    FOREIGN KEY (current_cycle_id) REFERENCES selection_cycle(cycle_id);

CREATE TABLE selection_request (
    request_id      UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    cycle_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES selection_cycle(cycle_id),
    job_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES job(job_id),
    provider_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES provider_profile(provider_id),
    source_job_version_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES job_version(job_version_id),
    source_response_version_id UUID REFERENCES response_version(response_version_id),
    terms_snapshot  JSONB,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING'
                    CHECK (status IN ('PENDING', 'ACCEPTED', 'DECLINED', 'EXPIRED', 'CLOSED')),
    issued_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at      TIMESTAMPTZ, -- B5 §2: 48 hours unless terminal earlier
    terminal_reason TEXT,
    -- X-01: Selection Request.job_id equals Selection Cycle.job_id
    -- Enforced via FK + application logic
    CONSTRAINT uq_one_pending_per_provider_cycle
        UNIQUE (cycle_id, provider_id) -- at most one pending per provider per cycle
);

-- ==============================================================
-- SELECTION ACCEPTANCE & ASSIGNMENT (EXP-05 — CRITICAL)
-- ==============================================================

CREATE TABLE selection_acceptance (
    acceptance_id   UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    request_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES selection_request(request_id),
    provider_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES provider_profile(provider_id),
    accepted_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL,
    result          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (result IN ('WON', 'LOST')),
    -- X-02: Selection Acceptance.provider_id equals Selection Request.provider_id
    -- Enforced at application level within the acceptance transaction
    CONSTRAINT uq_acceptance_idempotency UNIQUE (idempotency_key)
);

CREATE TABLE assignment (
    assignment_id   UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    job_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES job(job_id),
    cycle_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES selection_cycle(cycle_id),
    winning_request_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES selection_request(request_id),
    provider_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES provider_profile(provider_id),
    organization_id UUID REFERENCES organization(organization_id),
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'
                    CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE', 'COMPLETED', 'CANCELLED')),
    formed_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    terminal_at     TIMESTAMPTZ,
    terminal_reason TEXT,
    -- X-03: Assignment job, cycle, Provider equal winning Request's
    -- Enforced at application level within acceptance transaction
    CONSTRAINT uq_assignment_request UNIQUE (winning_request_id)
);

-- INV-05: A Job may have at most one active Assignment
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_one_active_assignment_per_job
    ON assignment (job_id)
    WHERE status = 'ACTIVE';

ALTER TABLE job ADD CONSTRAINT fk_job_active_assignment
    FOREIGN KEY (active_assignment_id) REFERENCES assignment(assignment_id);

-- ==============================================================
-- INFRASTRUCTURE (EXP-06 idempotency, EXP-07 outbox, EXP-13 audit)
-- ==============================================================

CREATE TABLE idempotency_record (
    key             TEXT NOT NULL,
    actor_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES identity(identity_id),
    payload_hash    TEXT NOT NULL,
    response_code   INTEGER NOT NULL,
    response_body   JSONB,
    result_reference TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (key, actor_id) -- actor-bound key scope
);

CREATE TABLE outbox_work_item (
    work_item_id    UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    work_type       TEXT NOT NULL,
    aggregate_type  TEXT NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id    UUID NOT NULL,
    payload         JSONB NOT NULL,
    dedupe_key      TEXT,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING'
                    CHECK (status IN ('PENDING', 'LOCKED', 'COMPLETED', 'FAILED')),
    attempts        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    max_attempts    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 5,
    available_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    locked_until    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_error      TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT uq_dedupe UNIQUE (dedupe_key)
);

-- Index for worker polling (EXP-07)
CREATE INDEX idx_outbox_pending
    ON outbox_work_item (available_at)
    WHERE status = 'PENDING';

CREATE TABLE business_event (
    event_id        UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    event_type      TEXT NOT NULL,
    aggregate_type  TEXT NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id    UUID NOT NULL,
    actor_id        UUID,
    payload         JSONB NOT NULL,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    correlation_id  TEXT
);

-- Append-only: no UPDATE or DELETE in application code

-- ==============================================================
-- MEDIA ASSET (EXP-08 object storage)
-- ==============================================================

CREATE TABLE media_asset (
    media_id        UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    owner_context   TEXT NOT NULL, -- 'JOB', 'MESSAGE', 'PROFILE', etc.
    owner_id        UUID NOT NULL,
    storage_key     TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, -- generated, never user filename
    content_type    TEXT NOT NULL, -- detected server-side
    size_bytes      BIGINT NOT NULL,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING'
                    CHECK (status IN ('PENDING', 'VALIDATED', 'REJECTED', 'PUBLISHED', 'ORPHANED')),
    sensitivity     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ORDINARY'
                    CHECK (sensitivity IN ('ORDINARY', 'EVIDENCE')),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    validated_at    TIMESTAMPTZ
);

COMMIT;

-- Migration 000002: Multi-step column evolution on outbox_work_item
-- Local Services Marketplace — Technical Stack Validation
--
-- Tests real-world safe schema evolution (EXP-03):
-- 1. Add nullable column
-- 2. Demonstrate compatibility with earlier state
-- 3. Backfill existing rows
-- 4. Add constraint and default
-- 5. Make NOT NULL after backfill
--
-- This is the domain-grounded V2 migration specified by the founder.

BEGIN;

-- Step 1: Add nullable payload_version column
ALTER TABLE outbox_work_item
    ADD COLUMN payload_version INTEGER;

-- Step 2: At this point, existing rows have NULL payload_version.
-- The application code from V1 is compatible (it does not reference this column).
-- New inserts will also have NULL until backfill + default.

-- Step 3: Backfill existing rows with version 1
UPDATE outbox_work_item
    SET payload_version = 1
    WHERE payload_version IS NULL;

-- Step 4: Add DEFAULT and CHECK constraint
ALTER TABLE outbox_work_item
    ALTER COLUMN payload_version SET DEFAULT 1;

ALTER TABLE outbox_work_item
    ADD CONSTRAINT chk_payload_version_positive
    CHECK (payload_version >= 1);

-- Step 5: Now that all rows are backfilled, make NOT NULL
ALTER TABLE outbox_work_item
    ALTER COLUMN payload_version SET NOT NULL;

COMMIT;

# Taskfile.ps1 — PowerShell task runner for Local Services Marketplace validation
# Replaces Makefile (make not available on this Windows machine).
# Usage: .\Taskfile.ps1 <command>
#
# All Go commands run inside the pinned golang container.

param(
    [Parameter(Position=0)]
    [string]$Command = "help"
)

$GO_IMAGE = "golang@sha256:9d2f36f06329b2a141b9db99ffa32765cf695ee57b813ca29e245e8670bcbfff"

function Invoke-Go {
    param([string]$Args)
    docker run --rm -v "${PWD}:/app" -w /app $GO_IMAGE $Args.Split(" ")
}

function Invoke-GoNetwork {
    # Same as Invoke-Go but with network access to reach PG/MinIO on host
    param([string]$Args)
    docker run --rm --network host -v "${PWD}:/app" -w /app $GO_IMAGE $Args.Split(" ")
}

switch ($Command) {
    "build" {
        Write-Host "Building all packages..."
        Invoke-Go "go build ./..."
    }
    "build-api" {
        Write-Host "Building API server..."
        Invoke-Go "go build -trimpath -ldflags=-s -w -o /dev/null ./cmd/api"
    }
    "build-worker" {
        Write-Host "Building worker..."
        Invoke-Go "go build -trimpath -ldflags=-s -w -o /dev/null ./cmd/worker"
    }
    "test" {
        Write-Host "Running tests..."
        Invoke-GoNetwork "go test -v -count=1 ./..."
    }
    "test-race" {
        Write-Host "Running tests with race detector..."
        Invoke-GoNetwork "go test -race -v -count=1 ./..."
    }
    "vet" {
        Write-Host "Running go vet..."
        Invoke-Go "go vet ./..."
    }
    "tidy" {
        Write-Host "Tidying modules..."
        Invoke-Go "go mod tidy"
    }
    "lint" {
        Write-Host "Running staticcheck..."
        docker run --rm -v "${PWD}:/app" -w /app $GO_IMAGE sh -c "go install honnef.co/go/tools/cmd/staticcheck@latest && staticcheck ./..."
    }
    "vulncheck" {
        Write-Host "Running govulncheck..."
        docker run --rm -v "${PWD}:/app" -w /app $GO_IMAGE sh -c "go install golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@latest && govulncheck ./..."
    }
    "up" {
        Write-Host "Starting default services (PG + MinIO)..."
        docker compose up -d
    }
    "down" {
        Write-Host "Stopping all services..."
        docker compose down
        docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.search.yml down
    }
    "up-typesense" {
        Write-Host "Starting with Typesense..."
        docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.search.yml --profile typesense up -d
    }
    "up-meilisearch" {
        Write-Host "Starting with Meilisearch..."
        docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.search.yml --profile meilisearch up -d
    }
    "migrate" {
        Write-Host "Running migrations..."
        # We use host network so we can reach localhost:5432
        docker run --rm --network host -v "${PWD}/migrations:/migrations" migrate/migrate:v4.18.2 -path=/migrations/ -database postgres://lsm:lsm_dev_only@localhost:5432/lsm?sslmode=disable up
    }
    "generate-db" {
        Write-Host "Generating DB models..."
        docker run --rm -v "${PWD}:/src" -w /src sqlc/sqlc:1.27.0 generate
    }
    "psql" {
        Write-Host "Connecting to PostgreSQL..."
        docker exec -it lsm-postgres psql -U lsm -d lsm
    }
    "generate-api" {
        Write-Host "Generating Go server interface from OpenAPI..."
        docker run --rm -v "${PWD}:/app" -w /app $GO_IMAGE sh -c "go install github.com/oapi-codegen/oapi-codegen/v2/cmd/oapi-codegen@latest && mkdir -p internal/marketplace/jobapi/gen && oapi-codegen -generate types,chi-server -package gen openapi/marketplace.yaml > internal/marketplace/jobapi/gen/api.gen.go"
    }
    "generate-client" {
        Write-Host "Generating TypeScript client types..."
        # Using npx openapi-typescript as it's the standard for TS types from OpenAPI
        npx openapi-typescript openapi/marketplace.yaml -o evidence/exp-02-http-openapi/client.d.ts
    }
    "clean" {
        Write-Host "Removing volumes and containers..."
        docker compose down -v
        docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.search.yml down -v
    }
    "help" {
        Write-Host @"
Taskfile.ps1 — Local Services Marketplace validation task runner

Usage: .\Taskfile.ps1 <command>

Go commands (containerized):
  build          Build all Go packages
  build-api      Build API server binary
  build-worker   Build worker binary
  test           Run all tests
  test-race      Run tests with race detector
  vet            Run go vet
  tidy           Run go mod tidy
  lint           Run staticcheck
  vulncheck      Run govulncheck

Infrastructure:
  up             Start PG + MinIO
  down           Stop all services
  up-typesense   Start PG + MinIO + Typesense
  up-meilisearch Start PG + MinIO + Meilisearch
  migrate        Run database migrations
  psql           Connect to PostgreSQL shell
  clean          Remove all containers and volumes
"@
    }
    default {
        Write-Host "Unknown command: $Command. Run .\Taskfile.ps1 help"
    }
}

# Build stage
FROM golang@sha256:9d2f36f06329b2a141b9db99ffa32765cf695ee57b813ca29e245e8670bcbfff AS builder

WORKDIR /app

# Cache dependencies
COPY go.mod go.sum ./
RUN go mod download

# Copy source
COPY . .

# Build API server
RUN CGO_ENABLED=0 GOOS=linux go build -trimpath -ldflags="-s -w" -o /bin/api ./cmd/api

# Runtime stage — minimal non-root image
FROM gcr.io/distroless/static-debian12:nonroot

COPY --from=builder /bin/api /bin/api
COPY --from=builder /bin/worker /bin/worker

USER nonroot:nonroot

ENTRYPOINT ["/bin/api"]

package db_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgconn"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgtype"
	"github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/marketplace/db"
	platformdb "github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/platform/db"
)

// TestConcurrency_FirstValidAcceptance validates that under concurrent
// load, only one Provider can successfully accept a Selection Request
// for a given Job Cycle (INV-05 & X-13).
func TestConcurrency_FirstValidAcceptance(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	pool, err := platformdb.Open(ctx, "postgres://lsm:lsm_dev_only@localhost:5432/lsm?sslmode=disable")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to connect: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	queries := db.New(pool)

	// 1. Setup shared Job and Cycle
	jobID := newUUID()
	custID := newUUID()
	marketID := newUUID()
	cycleID := newUUID()
	sourceJobVersionID := newUUID()

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO identity (identity_id, display_name) VALUES ($1, $2)`, custID, "Concurrency Customer")
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert identity failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO customer_participation (customer_id, identity_id, status) VALUES ($1, $2, 'ACTIVE')`, custID, custID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert customer failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO market (market_id, name, status) VALUES ($1, 'Concurrency Market', 'ACTIVE')`, marketID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert market failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO job (job_id, customer_id, market_id, status) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 'PUBLISHED')`, jobID, custID, marketID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert job failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO job_version (job_version_id, job_id, version_no, description) VALUES ($1, $2, 1, 'Concurrent job')`, sourceJobVersionID, jobID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert job version failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO selection_cycle (cycle_id, job_id, cycle_no) VALUES ($1, $2, 1)`, cycleID, jobID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert selection cycle failed: %v", err) }

	// 2. Setup 5 concurrent Providers and their specific Requests
	const numProviders = 5
	requests := make([]pgtype.UUID, numProviders)
	providers := make([]pgtype.UUID, numProviders)

	for i := 0; i < numProviders; i++ {
		pID := newUUID()
		reqID := newUUID()
		providers[i] = pID
		requests[i] = reqID

		_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO identity (identity_id, display_name) VALUES ($1, $2)`, pID, "Concurrent Provider")
		if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert prov identity failed: %v", err) }
		_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO provider_profile (provider_id, individual_identity_id, owner_kind, display_name, status, visibility) VALUES ($1, $2, 'INDIVIDUAL', 'Prov', 'ACTIVE', 'VISIBLE')`, pID, pID)
		if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert provider failed: %v", err) }

		_, err = queries.InsertSelectionRequest(ctx, db.InsertSelectionRequestParams{
			RequestID:          reqID,
			CycleID:            cycleID,
			JobID:              jobID,
			ProviderID:         pID,
			SourceJobVersionID: sourceJobVersionID,
			TermsSnapshot:      []byte(`{}`),
			Status:             "PENDING",
			IssuedAt:           time.Now(),
			ExpiresAt:          newTime(time.Now().Add(24 * time.Hour)),
		})
		if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert request failed: %v", err) }
	}

	// 3. Fire concurrent acceptance transactions
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	results := make([]error, numProviders)

	// Wait channel to fire them as close together as possible
	startCh := make(chan struct{})

	for i := 0; i < numProviders; i++ {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func(idx int) {
			defer wg.Done()
			reqID := requests[idx]
			provID := providers[idx]
			accID := newUUID()
			assignID := newUUID()

			<-startCh // Wait for the signal

			// Attempt the Acceptance Transaction
			err := pgx.BeginFunc(ctx, pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
				txQueries := queries.WithTx(tx)

				// In real logic, we'd SELECT FOR UPDATE the selection_cycle here if needed,
				// but PostgreSQL unique constraints should protect us even without explicit locking.

				// 1. Insert Acceptance (WON)
				_, err := txQueries.InsertSelectionAcceptance(ctx, db.InsertSelectionAcceptanceParams{
					AcceptanceID:   accID,
					RequestID:      reqID,
					ProviderID:     provID,
					AcceptedAt:     time.Now(),
					IdempotencyKey: accID.String(), // unique for this attempt
					Result:         "WON",
				})
				if err != nil {
					return err
				}

				// 2. Insert Assignment
				// INV-05: A Job may have at most one active Assignment.
				// This should throw a unique constraint violation for all but one transaction!
				_, err = txQueries.InsertAssignment(ctx, db.InsertAssignmentParams{
					AssignmentID:     assignID,
					JobID:            jobID,
					CycleID:          cycleID,
					WinningRequestID: reqID,
					ProviderID:       provID,
					Status:           "ACTIVE",
					FormedAt:         time.Now(),
				})
				if err != nil {
					return err
				}

				return nil
			})

			results[idx] = err
		}(i)
	}

	// Fire!
	close(startCh)
	wg.Wait()

	// 4. Verify results
	successCount := 0
	conflictCount := 0

	for _, err := range results {
		if err == nil {
			successCount++
		} else {
			var pgErr *pgconn.PgError
			if errors.As(err, &pgErr) && pgErr.Code == "23505" {
				// 23505 is unique_violation
				if strings.Contains(pgErr.ConstraintName, "uq_one_active_assignment_per_job") {
					conflictCount++
				} else {
					t.Errorf("unexpected constraint violated: %s", pgErr.ConstraintName)
				}
			} else {
				t.Errorf("unexpected error from transaction: %v", err)
			}
		}
	}

	if successCount != 1 {
		t.Errorf("expected exactly 1 winner, got %d", successCount)
	}
	if conflictCount != numProviders-1 {
		t.Errorf("expected %d conflicts, got %d", numProviders-1, conflictCount)
	}

	t.Logf("Concurrency test passed: %d winners, %d rejected by constraint", successCount, conflictCount)
}

package db_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgconn"
	"github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/marketplace/db"
	platformdb "github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/platform/db"
)

// TestIdempotency_DuplicateAcceptance validates that submitting the same
// action (e.g., Acceptance) twice with the same idempotency key
// results in a catchable unique constraint violation, allowing the application
// to gracefully absorb network retries.
func TestIdempotency_DuplicateAcceptance(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	pool, err := platformdb.Open(ctx, "postgres://lsm:lsm_dev_only@localhost:5432/lsm?sslmode=disable")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to connect: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	queries := db.New(pool)

	// 1. Setup minimal dependencies
	reqID := newUUID()
	providerID := newUUID()
	accID := newUUID()
	idempotencyKey := "idem-test-key-" + newUUID().String()

	// Insert identity and provider profile
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO identity (identity_id, display_name) VALUES ($1, $2)`, providerID, "Idempotent Provider")
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert identity failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO provider_profile (provider_id, individual_identity_id, owner_kind, display_name, status, visibility) VALUES ($1, $2, 'INDIVIDUAL', 'Prov', 'ACTIVE', 'VISIBLE')`, providerID, providerID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert provider failed: %v", err) }

	// Note: We don't need a full Job/Request tree if we are just testing the
	// idempotency key constraint on the acceptance table, but we do need the
	// request to exist to satisfy FK.
	jobID := newUUID()
	custID := newUUID()
	marketID := newUUID()
	cycleID := newUUID()
	sourceJobVersionID := newUUID()

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO identity (identity_id, display_name) VALUES ($1, $2)`, custID, "Idemp Customer")
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert identity failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO customer_participation (customer_id, identity_id, status) VALUES ($1, $2, 'ACTIVE')`, custID, custID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert customer failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO market (market_id, name, status) VALUES ($1, 'Idemp Market', 'ACTIVE')`, marketID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert market failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO job (job_id, customer_id, market_id, status) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 'PUBLISHED')`, jobID, custID, marketID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert job failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO job_version (job_version_id, job_id, version_no, description) VALUES ($1, $2, 1, 'Idemp job')`, sourceJobVersionID, jobID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert job version failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO selection_cycle (cycle_id, job_id, cycle_no) VALUES ($1, $2, 1)`, cycleID, jobID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert selection cycle failed: %v", err) }
	_, err = queries.InsertSelectionRequest(ctx, db.InsertSelectionRequestParams{
		RequestID:          reqID,
		CycleID:            cycleID,
		JobID:              jobID,
		ProviderID:         providerID,
		SourceJobVersionID: sourceJobVersionID,
		TermsSnapshot:      []byte(`{}`),
		Status:             "PENDING",
		IssuedAt:           time.Now(),
		ExpiresAt:          newTime(time.Now().Add(24 * time.Hour)),
	})
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert request failed: %v", err) }

	// 2. First attempt (should succeed)
	_, err = queries.InsertSelectionAcceptance(ctx, db.InsertSelectionAcceptanceParams{
		AcceptanceID:   accID,
		RequestID:      reqID,
		ProviderID:     providerID,
		AcceptedAt:     time.Now(),
		IdempotencyKey: idempotencyKey,
		Result:         "WON",
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("first insert failed: %v", err)
	}

	// 3. Second attempt with the SAME idempotency key (simulating network retry)
	_, err = queries.InsertSelectionAcceptance(ctx, db.InsertSelectionAcceptanceParams{
		AcceptanceID:   newUUID(), // New UUID, but same idempotency key
		RequestID:      reqID,
		ProviderID:     providerID,
		AcceptedAt:     time.Now(),
		IdempotencyKey: idempotencyKey,
		Result:         "WON",
	})

	// 4. Verify that the second attempt returns a specific error we can catch
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatalf("expected duplicate insert to fail, but it succeeded")
	}

	var pgErr *pgconn.PgError
	if errors.As(err, &pgErr) && pgErr.Code == "23505" {
		if pgErr.ConstraintName == "uq_acceptance_idempotency" {
			t.Logf("Successfully caught idempotency constraint violation: %s", pgErr.ConstraintName)
		} else {
			t.Errorf("expected uq_acceptance_idempotency violation, got %s", pgErr.ConstraintName)
		}
	} else {
		t.Errorf("expected pgconn.PgError with code 23505, got %v", err)
	}
}

package db_test

import (
	"context"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgtype"
	"github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/marketplace/db"
	platformdb "github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/platform/db"
)

// TestOutbox_WorkerPolling validates the outbox pattern (EXP-07).
// It verifies that multiple workers polling the outbox table concurrently
// correctly use FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED to grab separate items without
// blocking each other or double-processing.
func TestOutbox_WorkerPolling(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	pool, err := platformdb.Open(ctx, "postgres://lsm:lsm_dev_only@localhost:5432/lsm?sslmode=disable")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to connect: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	queries := db.New(pool)

	// 1. Insert 10 pending outbox items
	const numItems = 10
	for i := 0; i < numItems; i++ {
		itemID := newUUID()
		aggID := newUUID()
		_, err := queries.InsertOutboxItem(ctx, db.InsertOutboxItemParams{
			WorkItemID:    itemID,
			WorkType:      "SEND_EMAIL",
			AggregateType: "JOB",
			AggregateID:   aggID,
			Payload:       []byte(`{"msg":"test"}`),
			DedupeKey:     pgtype.Text{String: "dedupe-" + itemID.String(), Valid: true},
			Status:        "PENDING",
			AvailableAt:   time.Now().Add(-1 * time.Minute), // make it immediately available
		})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("failed to insert outbox item: %v", err)
		}
	}

	// 2. Spawn 3 concurrent workers to drain the queue
	const numWorkers = 3
	var wg sync.WaitGroup

	// We'll track processed items to ensure no double processing
	var mu sync.Mutex
	processed := make(map[string]bool)

	worker := func(workerID int) {
		defer wg.Done()
		for {
			// Try to claim the next item
			item, err := queries.ClaimNextOutboxItem(ctx, db.ClaimNextOutboxItemParams{
				AvailableAt: time.Now(),
				LockedUntil: newTime(time.Now().Add(5 * time.Minute)),
			})
			
			if err != nil {
				// sql.ErrNoRows in pgx is pgx.ErrNoRows
				if err.Error() == "no rows in result set" {
					// Queue empty
					return
				}
				t.Errorf("worker %d error claiming: %v", workerID, err)
				return
			}

			// Successfully claimed!
			mu.Lock()
			idStr := item.WorkItemID.String()
			if processed[idStr] {
				t.Errorf("FATAL: worker %d claimed an already processed item %s", workerID, idStr)
			}
			processed[idStr] = true
			mu.Unlock()

			// Simulate work
			time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)

			// Mark completed
			_, err = queries.MarkOutboxItemCompleted(ctx, db.MarkOutboxItemCompletedParams{
				WorkItemID:  item.WorkItemID,
				CompletedAt: newTime(time.Now()),
			})
			if err != nil {
				t.Errorf("worker %d failed to mark complete: %v", workerID, err)
			}
		}
	}

	for i := 0; i < numWorkers; i++ {
		wg.Add(1)
		go worker(i)
	}

	wg.Wait()

	// 3. Verify exactly 10 unique items were processed
	if len(processed) < numItems {
		t.Errorf("expected at least %d items to be processed, got %d", numItems, len(processed))
	}
	t.Logf("Outbox pattern successful: 3 workers safely drained %d items using SKIP LOCKED", len(processed))
}
