package db_test

import (
	"context"
	"os"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgtype"
	"github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/marketplace/db"
	platformdb "github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/platform/db"
)

func newUUID() pgtype.UUID {
	u := uuid.New()
	return pgtype.UUID{Bytes: u, Valid: true}
}

func newTime(t time.Time) pgtype.Timestamptz {
	return pgtype.Timestamptz{Time: t, Valid: true}
}

func TestTransactionAcceptance_Success(t *testing.T) {
	dsn := os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL")
	if dsn == "" {
		dsn = "postgres://lsm:lsm_dev_only@localhost:5432/lsm?sslmode=disable"
	}

	ctx := context.Background()
	pool, err := platformdb.Open(ctx, dsn)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to connect: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	queries := db.New(pool)

	jobID := newUUID()
	reqID := newUUID()
	accID := newUUID()
	custID := newUUID()
	marketID := newUUID()
	providerID := newUUID()
	cycleID := newUUID()
	assignID := newUUID()

	// Setup foreign key dependencies
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO identity (identity_id, display_name) VALUES ($1, $2)`, custID, "Customer")
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert identity failed: %v", err) }
	
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO customer_participation (customer_id, identity_id, status) VALUES ($1, $2, 'ACTIVE')`, custID, custID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert customer failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO market (market_id, name, status) VALUES ($1, 'Market 1', 'ACTIVE')`, marketID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert market failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO identity (identity_id, display_name) VALUES ($1, $2)`, providerID, "Provider")
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert prov identity failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO provider_profile (provider_id, individual_identity_id, owner_kind, display_name, status, visibility) VALUES ($1, $2, 'INDIVIDUAL', 'Prov', 'ACTIVE', 'VISIBLE')`, providerID, providerID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert provider failed: %v", err) }

	// Setup: insert job and selection request
	_, err = queries.InsertJob(ctx, db.InsertJobParams{
		JobID:       jobID,
		CustomerID:  custID,
		MarketID:    marketID,
		Status:      "PUBLISHED",
		PublishedAt: newTime(time.Now()),
		ExpiresAt:   newTime(time.Now().Add(5 * 24 * time.Hour)),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("setup insert job failed: %v", err)
	}

	// Insert Job Version to satisfy source_job_version_id FK
	sourceJobVersionID := newUUID()
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO job_version (job_version_id, job_id, version_no, description) VALUES ($1, $2, 1, 'Test description')`, sourceJobVersionID, jobID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert job version failed: %v", err) }

	// Insert Selection Cycle to satisfy cycle_id FK
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO selection_cycle (cycle_id, job_id, cycle_no) VALUES ($1, $2, 1)`, cycleID, jobID)
	if err != nil { t.Fatalf("insert selection cycle failed: %v", err) }

	_, err = queries.InsertSelectionRequest(ctx, db.InsertSelectionRequestParams{
		RequestID:          reqID,
		CycleID:            cycleID,
		JobID:              jobID,
		ProviderID:         providerID,
		SourceJobVersionID: sourceJobVersionID,
		TermsSnapshot:      []byte(`{}`),
		Status:             "PENDING",
		IssuedAt:           time.Now(),
		ExpiresAt:          newTime(time.Now().Add(24 * time.Hour)),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("setup insert request failed: %v", err)
	}

	// EXP-04 c) Explicit transaction for Acceptance Use Case
	tx, err := pool.Begin(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("begin tx failed: %v", err)
	}
	defer tx.Rollback(ctx)

	txQueries := queries.WithTx(tx)

	// 1. Insert acceptance
	_, err = txQueries.InsertSelectionAcceptance(ctx, db.InsertSelectionAcceptanceParams{
		AcceptanceID:   accID,
		RequestID:      reqID,
		ProviderID:     providerID,
		AcceptedAt:     time.Now(),
		IdempotencyKey: accID.String(),
		Result:         "WON",
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("insert acceptance failed: %v", err)
	}

	// 2. Insert assignment
	_, err = txQueries.InsertAssignment(ctx, db.InsertAssignmentParams{
		AssignmentID:     assignID,
		JobID:            jobID,
		CycleID:          cycleID,
		WinningRequestID: reqID,
		ProviderID:       providerID,
		Status:           "ACTIVE",
		FormedAt:         time.Now(),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("insert assignment failed: %v", err)
	}

	// 3. Update job status
	_, err = txQueries.UpdateJobStatus(ctx, db.UpdateJobStatusParams{
		JobID:              jobID,
		Status:             "ASSIGNED",
		ActiveAssignmentID: assignID,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("update job failed: %v", err)
	}

	// Commit transaction
	if err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("commit tx failed: %v", err)
	}

	// Verify Job Status
	savedJob, err := queries.FetchJob(ctx, jobID)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("fetch job failed: %v", err)
	}
	if savedJob.Status != "ASSIGNED" {
		t.Errorf("expected job status ASSIGNED, got %s", savedJob.Status)
	}
}

func TestDynamicQueryPattern(t *testing.T) {
	dsn := os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL")
	if dsn == "" {
		dsn = "postgres://lsm:lsm_dev_only@localhost:5432/lsm?sslmode=disable"
	}

	ctx := context.Background()
	pool, err := platformdb.Open(ctx, dsn)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to connect: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	marketID := newUUID()

	query := `
		SELECT job_id, status 
		FROM job 
		WHERE market_id = $1 
		  AND status = ANY($2::text[])
		LIMIT 10
	`
	rows, err := pool.Query(ctx, query, marketID, []string{"PUBLISHED", "ASSIGNED"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("dynamic query failed: %v", err)
	}
	defer rows.Close()

	for rows.Next() {
		var jobID pgtype.UUID
		var status string
		if err := rows.Scan(&jobID, &status); err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("scan failed: %v", err)
		}
	}
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("rows iteration failed: %v", err)
	}
}

HISTORICAL FILE: internal/media/s3.go
COMMIT: 368535e0d1d3b2f7421e9316a4511b97f50a010c

package media

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/aws"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/config"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/credentials"
	"github.com/aws/aws-sdk-go-v2/service/s3"
)

type S3Storage struct {
	client     *s3.Client
	presignCl  *s3.PresignClient
	bucketName string
}

func NewS3Storage(ctx context.Context, endpoint, region, key, secret, bucket string) (*S3Storage, error) {
	customResolver := aws.EndpointResolverWithOptionsFunc(func(service, r string, options ...interface{}) (aws.Endpoint, error) {
		if service == s3.ServiceID {
			return aws.Endpoint{
				PartitionID:       "aws",
				URL:               endpoint,
				SigningRegion:     region,
				HostnameImmutable: true, // Crucial for MinIO/Path style routing
			}, nil
		}
		return aws.Endpoint{}, fmt.Errorf("unknown endpoint requested")
	})

	cfg, err := config.LoadDefaultConfig(ctx,
		config.WithRegion(region),
		config.WithEndpointResolverWithOptions(customResolver),
		config.WithCredentialsProvider(credentials.NewStaticCredentialsProvider(key, secret, "")),
	)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	client := s3.NewFromConfig(cfg, func(o *s3.Options) {
		o.UsePathStyle = true // Crucial for MinIO
	})

	return &S3Storage{
		client:     client,
		presignCl:  s3.NewPresignClient(client),
		bucketName: bucket,
	}, nil
}

func (s *S3Storage) CreateBucketForTest(ctx context.Context) error {
	_, err := s.client.CreateBucket(ctx, &s3.CreateBucketInput{
		Bucket: aws.String(s.bucketName),
	})
	return err
}

func (s *S3Storage) GenerateUploadURL(ctx context.Context, key string, contentType string, maxSize int64, expiry time.Duration) (string, error) {
	req, err := s.presignCl.PresignPutObject(ctx, &s3.PutObjectInput{
		Bucket:        aws.String(s.bucketName),
		Key:           aws.String(key),
		ContentType:   aws.String(contentType),
	}, func(po *s3.PresignOptions) {
		po.Expires = expiry
	})
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return req.URL, nil
}

func (s *S3Storage) GenerateDownloadURL(ctx context.Context, key string, expiry time.Duration) (string, error) {
	req, err := s.presignCl.PresignGetObject(ctx, &s3.GetObjectInput{
		Bucket: aws.String(s.bucketName),
		Key:    aws.String(key),
	}, func(po *s3.PresignOptions) {
		po.Expires = expiry
	})
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return req.URL, nil
}

func (s *S3Storage) HeadObject(ctx context.Context, key string) (int64, string, error) {
	res, err := s.client.HeadObject(ctx, &s3.HeadObjectInput{
		Bucket: aws.String(s.bucketName),
		Key:    aws.String(key),
	})
	if err != nil {
		return 0, "", err
	}
	
	ctype := ""
	if res.ContentType != nil {
		ctype = *res.ContentType
	}
	
	var size int64 = 0
	if res.ContentLength != nil {
		size = *res.ContentLength
	}
	return size, ctype, nil
}

func (s *S3Storage) DeleteObject(ctx context.Context, key string) error {
	_, err := s.client.DeleteObject(ctx, &s3.DeleteObjectInput{
		Bucket: aws.String(s.bucketName),
		Key:    aws.String(key),
	})
	return err
}

package media_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/media"
)

// TestS3Storage_UploadDownload validates that the MinIO/S3 container
// can correctly issue presigned URLs for direct client uploads,
// and that those uploads can be downloaded securely (EXP-08).
func TestS3Storage_UploadDownload(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()

	endpoint := os.Getenv("MINIO_ENDPOINT")
	if endpoint == "" {
		endpoint = "http://127.0.0.1:9000"
	}
	accessKey := os.Getenv("MINIO_ROOT_USER")
	if accessKey == "" {
		accessKey = "minioadmin"
	}
	secretKey := os.Getenv("MINIO_ROOT_PASSWORD")
	if secretKey == "" {
		secretKey = "minioadmin"
	}

	storage, err := media.NewS3Storage(ctx, endpoint, "us-east-1", accessKey, secretKey, "lsm-media")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to init storage: %v", err)
	}

	// Create bucket if it doesn't exist (since MinIO starts empty)
	err = storage.CreateBucketForTest(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		// Ignore BucketAlreadyOwnedByYou error
		if !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "BucketAlreadyOwnedByYou") && !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "BucketAlreadyExists") {
			t.Fatalf("failed to create test bucket: %v", err)
		}
	}

	testKey := "test/job-media-123.jpg"
	testPayload := []byte("fake-jpeg-content")

	// 1. Generate PUT URL
	uploadURL, err := storage.GenerateUploadURL(ctx, testKey, "image/jpeg", 5*1024*1024, 15*time.Minute)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to generate upload url: %v", err)
	}

	if !strings.Contains(uploadURL, testKey) {
		t.Errorf("expected upload URL to contain key %s, got %s", testKey, uploadURL)
	}

	// 2. Perform the PUT request directly to MinIO (simulating frontend client)
	req, err := http.NewRequest(http.MethodPut, uploadURL, bytes.NewReader(testPayload))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to create http request: %v", err)
	}
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "image/jpeg")

	client := &http.Client{Timeout: 5 * time.Second}
	resp, err := client.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("PUT request to MinIO failed: %v", err)
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()

	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		body, _ := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
		t.Fatalf("MinIO rejected upload with status %d: %s", resp.StatusCode, string(body))
	}

	// 3. Verify HeadObject via backend
	size, ctype, err := storage.HeadObject(ctx, testKey)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("HeadObject failed: %v", err)
	}
	if size != int64(len(testPayload)) {
		t.Errorf("expected size %d, got %d", len(testPayload), size)
	}
	if ctype != "image/jpeg" {
		t.Errorf("expected content type image/jpeg, got %s", ctype)
	}

	// 4. Generate GET URL and verify download
	downloadURL, err := storage.GenerateDownloadURL(ctx, testKey, 15*time.Minute)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to generate download url: %v", err)
	}

	getResp, err := client.Get(downloadURL)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("GET request to MinIO failed: %v", err)
	}
	defer getResp.Body.Close()

	if getResp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("MinIO rejected download with status %d", getResp.StatusCode)
	}

	downloaded, err := io.ReadAll(getResp.Body)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed reading download body: %v", err)
	}

	if !bytes.Equal(downloaded, testPayload) {
		t.Errorf("download payload did not match upload")
	}

	// 5. Cleanup
	err = storage.DeleteObject(ctx, testKey)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("DeleteObject failed: %v", err)
	}

	t.Logf("S3 Presigned URL Upload and Download strictly validated via local MinIO")
}

package discovery

import (
	"context"
	"bytes"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
)

// SearchEngine represents a candidate search backend.
type SearchEngine interface {
	Name() string
	Index(ctx context.Context, jobs []JobDocument) error
	Search(ctx context.Context, query string) ([]string, error)
	Teardown(ctx context.Context) error
}

// JobDocument is the flattened representation of a Job used for search indexing.
type JobDocument struct {
	ID          string `json:"id"`
	Title       string `json:"title"`
	Description string `json:"description"`
}

// Evaluate engines by calculating MRR (Mean Reciprocal Rank) for a set of queries.
func Evaluate(ctx context.Context, engines []SearchEngine, dataset []JobDocument, queries map[string]string) map[string]float64 {
	results := make(map[string]float64)

	for _, engine := range engines {
		fmt.Printf("Indexing %d documents into %s...\n", len(dataset), engine.Name())
		engine.Index(ctx, dataset)
		
		time.Sleep(1 * time.Second) // allow for async indexing to settle

		var totalReciprocalRank float64 = 0
		
		for query, expectedID := range queries {
			docs, _ := engine.Search(ctx, query)
			rank := 0
			for i, id := range docs {
				if id == expectedID {
					rank = i + 1
					break
				}
			}
			
			if rank > 0 {
				totalReciprocalRank += 1.0 / float64(rank)
			}
		}
		
		mrr := totalReciprocalRank / float64(len(queries))
		results[engine.Name()] = mrr
		fmt.Printf("Engine %s MRR: %.4f\n", engine.Name(), mrr)
	}

	return results
}

// --- Postgres Engine ---
type PostgresEngine struct {
	Pool *pgxpool.Pool
}

func (p *PostgresEngine) Name() string { return "PostgreSQL (pg_trgm)" }
func (p *PostgresEngine) Index(ctx context.Context, jobs []JobDocument) error {
	p.Pool.Exec(ctx, `CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm`)
	p.Pool.Exec(ctx, `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS search_eval (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, title TEXT, description TEXT)`)
	p.Pool.Exec(ctx, `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS trgm_idx ON search_eval USING gin (description gin_trgm_ops)`)
	
	for _, j := range jobs {
		p.Pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO search_eval (id, title, description) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)`, j.ID, j.Title, j.Description)
	}
	return nil
}
func (p *PostgresEngine) Search(ctx context.Context, query string) ([]string, error) {
	rows, err := p.Pool.Query(ctx, `SELECT id FROM search_eval WHERE description % $1 ORDER BY similarity(description, $1) DESC LIMIT 10`, query)
	if err != nil { return nil, err }
	defer rows.Close()
	var res []string
	for rows.Next() {
		var id string
		rows.Scan(&id)
		res = append(res, id)
	}
	return res, nil
}
func (p *PostgresEngine) Teardown(ctx context.Context) error {
	_, err := p.Pool.Exec(ctx, `DROP TABLE search_eval`)
	return err
}

// --- Typesense Engine (HTTP) ---
type TypesenseEngine struct {
	URL    string
	APIKey string
}

func (t *TypesenseEngine) Name() string { return "Typesense" }
func (t *TypesenseEngine) Index(ctx context.Context, jobs []JobDocument) error {
	schema := `{
		"name": "jobs",
		"fields": [
			{"name": "id", "type": "string"},
			{"name": "title", "type": "string"},
			{"name": "description", "type": "string"}
		]
	}`
	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", t.URL+"/collections", bytes.NewBufferString(schema))
	req.Header.Set("X-TYPESENSE-API-KEY", t.APIKey)
	http.DefaultClient.Do(req)

	for _, j := range jobs {
		body, _ := json.Marshal(j)
		req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", t.URL+"/collections/jobs/documents", bytes.NewBuffer(body))
		req.Header.Set("X-TYPESENSE-API-KEY", t.APIKey)
		http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	}
	return nil
}
func (t *TypesenseEngine) Search(ctx context.Context, query string) ([]string, error) {
	url := fmt.Sprintf("%s/collections/jobs/documents/search?q=%s&query_by=description", t.URL, query)
	req, _ := http.NewRequest("GET", url, nil)
	req.Header.Set("X-TYPESENSE-API-KEY", t.APIKey)
	resp, err := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	if err != nil { return nil, err }
	defer resp.Body.Close()

	var res struct {
		Hits []struct {
			Document struct {
				ID string `json:"id"`
			} `json:"document"`
		} `json:"hits"`
	}
	json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&res)

	var ids []string
	for _, h := range res.Hits {
		ids = append(ids, h.Document.ID)
	}
	return ids, nil
}
func (t *TypesenseEngine) Teardown(ctx context.Context) error {
	req, _ := http.NewRequest("DELETE", t.URL+"/collections/jobs", nil)
	req.Header.Set("X-TYPESENSE-API-KEY", t.APIKey)
	http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	return nil
}

// --- Meilisearch Engine (HTTP) ---
type MeilisearchEngine struct {
	URL    string
	APIKey string
}

func (m *MeilisearchEngine) Name() string { return "Meilisearch" }
func (m *MeilisearchEngine) Index(ctx context.Context, jobs []JobDocument) error {
	body, _ := json.Marshal(jobs)
	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", m.URL+"/indexes/jobs/documents?primaryKey=id", bytes.NewBuffer(body))
	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+m.APIKey)
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	return nil
}
func (m *MeilisearchEngine) Search(ctx context.Context, query string) ([]string, error) {
	reqBody := fmt.Sprintf(`{"q": "%s", "limit": 10}`, query)
	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", m.URL+"/indexes/jobs/search", bytes.NewBufferString(reqBody))
	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+m.APIKey)
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	resp, err := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	if err != nil { return nil, err }
	defer resp.Body.Close()

	var res struct {
		Hits []struct {
			ID string `json:"id"`
		} `json:"hits"`
	}
	json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&res)

	var ids []string
	for _, h := range res.Hits {
		ids = append(ids, h.ID)
	}
	return ids, nil
}
func (m *MeilisearchEngine) Teardown(ctx context.Context) error {
	req, _ := http.NewRequest("DELETE", m.URL+"/indexes/jobs", nil)
	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+m.APIKey)
	http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	return nil
}

package discovery_test

import (
	"context"
	"os"
	"testing"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
	"github.com/localservices/marketplace/internal/discovery"
)

func TestSearchEvaluation(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()

	// 1. Setup Engines
	// PostgreSQL
	pool, err := pgxpool.New(ctx, "postgres://lsm:lsm_dev_only@localhost:5432/lsm?sslmode=disable")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("failed to connect to pg: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()
	pgEngine := &discovery.PostgresEngine{Pool: pool}

	// Typesense
	tsKey := os.Getenv("TYPESENSE_API_KEY")
	if tsKey == "" { tsKey = "ts_dev_key" }
	tsEngine := &discovery.TypesenseEngine{
		URL: "http://localhost:8108",
		APIKey: tsKey,
	}

	// Meilisearch
	msKey := os.Getenv("MEILI_MASTER_KEY")
	if msKey == "" { msKey = "ms_dev_key" }
	msEngine := &discovery.MeilisearchEngine{
		URL: "http://localhost:7700",
		APIKey: msKey,
	}

	engines := []discovery.SearchEngine{pgEngine, tsEngine, msEngine}

	// 2. Teardown before and after
	for _, engine := range engines {
		_ = engine.Teardown(ctx) // ignore error if table doesn't exist
	}
	defer func() {
		for _, engine := range engines {
			_ = engine.Teardown(ctx)
		}
	}()

	// 3. Dataset (Imperfect Queries and Fuzzy Text)
	dataset := []discovery.JobDocument{
		{ID: "j1", Title: "Fix leaky pipe", Description: "My kitchen sink pipe is leaking water everywhere. Need a plumber ASAP."},
		{ID: "j2", Title: "Install ceiling fan", Description: "Need an electrician to install a new ceiling fan in the master bedroom."},
		{ID: "j3", Title: "Mow lawn", Description: "Need someone to mow my front and back lawn. Grass is very long."},
		{ID: "j4", Title: "Fix roof leak", Description: "Water is coming through the roof during heavy rain. Need a roofer."},
		{ID: "j5", Title: "Assemble IKEA furniture", Description: "Need help building a large wardrobe and two bookshelves."},
		{ID: "j6", Title: "Paint living room", Description: "Looking for a painter to paint my living room walls and ceiling white."},
		{ID: "j7", Title: "Clean windows", Description: "Need all exterior windows washed on a 2-story house."},
		{ID: "j8", Title: "Repair drywall", Description: "There is a fist-sized hole in the drywall that needs patching and painting."},
		{ID: "j9", Title: "Unclog toilet", Description: "The guest bathroom toilet is completely blocked."},
		{ID: "j10", Title: "Mount TV", Description: "Need a 65 inch television mounted to the living room wall."},
	}

	// Queries mapped to expected expected document ID
	// Contains typos and fuzzy matches
	queries := map[string]string{
		"plumer for sink": "j1", // typo "plumer"
		"electrician fan": "j2",
		"cut grass": "j3", // synonym
		"roofing repair": "j4",
		"build shelf": "j5",
		"painter": "j6",
		"window washing": "j7",
		"fix wall hole": "j8",
		"blocked bathroom": "j9",
		"hang television": "j10",
	}

	// 4. Evaluate
	results := discovery.Evaluate(ctx, engines, dataset, queries)

	// 5. Assertions
	t.Logf("Search MRR Results:")
	for name, mrr := range results {
		t.Logf("%s: %.4f", name, mrr)
	}
	
	// Ensure at least one engine is decent
	if results["Typesense"] < 0.1 && results["Meilisearch"] < 0.1 && results["PostgreSQL (pg_trgm)"] < 0.1 {
		t.Errorf("All engines performed terribly.")
	}
}
